Title: A Manual of Doctrine: or, a Second Essay to bring into the Form of Question and Answer as well the Fundamental Doctrines, as the other Scripture-Knowledge, of the Protestant Congregations who for 300 Years past have been call'd The Brethren; written in High-Dutch by the Author of the First Essay, and now translated into English
Creator: Nikolaus Ludwig, Count von Zinzendorf (1700-1760), named on the title page only as 'the Author of the First Essay'; translator unnamed
Publisher: London: printed for James Hutton, at the Bible and Sun in Little Wild Street
Publication Date: 1742
Rights: Public Domain (published 1742, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Source: Internet Archive item bim_eighteenth-century_a-manual-of-doctrine-or_zinzendorf-nicolaus-lud_1742 (https://archive.org/details/bim_eighteenth-century_a-manual-of-doctrine-or_zinzendorf-nicolaus-lud_1742), the item's own bim_eighteenth-century_a-manual-of-doctrine-or_zinzendorf-nicolaus-lud_1742_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Internet Archive; host metadata date: 1742. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: b4180b9e9b688687a420ec45303403c45cf887e91f14fa4c43f612396bec242e.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. British Library microfilm scan; the title page is badly garbled, the numbered questions and answers readable with the long s kept.
Content note: Zinzendorf's catechism of 1742 in question-and-answer form, each answer a scripture text with its reference; the Brethren's teaching as it was meant to be learned.
